ONE-ARMED ROBBER: FULL OBJECT LIST
==============================================================================
Every Blueprint in the game files, from every map, read straight out of the pak.
1283 classes (incl. 9 level scripts), 16 structs, contents of 53 maps.
Engine (C++) classes are all in UUU_ObjectsDump.txt already (they live in the exe).

Layout per class:  Name : Parent : Grandparent ...    /Game/path
  components  name (component class)
  variables   type name   [replicated]
  maps        maps this class is placed on (load one with  open <map>  to summon it by short name)
  functions   Name(params) -> return   [Server/Client/Multicast = network RPC, Event, Pure]
Function locals and compiler-made functions (ExecuteUbergraph, BndEvt__) are left out.


LEVEL SCRIPTS (one per map; their events can be fired with  ce <EventName>  if ce works)  [9]
